arith + four mem ops, the three leaf jumps ret/brkB/contL, and **cref/clen**.

  `ofInt 64 v` into any scratch `t` (≠0,2), IL state preserved, plus an

  (`(ofInt hi)<<<12 + ofInt lo = ofInt (hi*4096+lo)`) are the reusable atoms.

  synth δ into T1 via `run_synth`, T0 preserved → `add T0,T0,T1`) loads

54	/-- Position-independent instruction stream: concrete instructions, label

56	inductive SymInstr where
57	  | ins   (i : Instr)                          -- concrete, position-independent
58	  | label (l : Nat)                            -- marker (0 bytes)
59	  | br    (c : Cond) (a b : Reg) (l : Nat)     -- branch (phys regs) to label
60	  | jmp   (l : Nat)                            -- jal x0, label
61	  | callf (f : Name)                           -- jal ra, function
62	  | cref  (d : Name)                           -- T0 := address of data object
63	deriving Repr

65	/-! ### Per-function lowering -/
66	
67	/-- Slot offset of IL register `r` (see frame picture above). -/
68	def slotOff (r : Reg) : Nat := 8 * (1 + r)
69	
70	/-- Load IL register `r`'s slot into physical register `t`
71	    (IL x0 is hardwired zero — never read its slot). -/
72	def loadSlot (r : Reg) (t : Reg) : List SymInstr :=
73	  if r = 0 then [.ins (.addi t 0 0)]
74	  else [.ins (.ld t SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r)))]
75	
76	/-- Store physical register `t` into IL register `r`'s slot
77	    (IL writes to x0 are discarded — emit nothing). -/
78	def storeSlot (r : Reg) (t : Reg) : List SymInstr :=
79	  if r = 0 then []
80	  else [.ins (.sd SP t (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r)))]
81	
82	/-- Fresh-label supply. -/
83	abbrev M := StateM Nat

32	/-! ## Call-marshalling emit helpers (arg loads / ret stores).
33	
34	    A `call` lowers to: one `loadSlotI arg[i] (A i)` per argument (always 1
35	    instruction each, incl. reg 0 → `addi (A i) x0 0`), then `jal RA` to the
36	    callee, then one `storeSlotI ret[i] (A i)` per RETURN (0 instructions for
37	    ret reg 0). These mirror the compiler's `lower` call arm exactly (`A i` =
38	    `x10+i`), validated by `matchesRealProg` below. -/
39	def marshalI (args : List Nat) : List Instr :=
40	  args.zipIdx.flatMap fun ri => loadSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)
41	
42	def retStoresI (rets : List Nat) : List Instr :=
43	  rets.zipIdx.flatMap fun ri => storeSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)
44	
45	/-- Each `loadSlotI` is one instruction, so `marshalI`'s length is the argument
46	    count (independent of the `A`-register indices). -/
47	theorem length_flatMap_loadSlotI (l : List (Nat × Nat)) :
48	    (l.flatMap fun ri => loadSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)).length = l.length := by
49	  induction l with
50	  | nil => rfl
51	  | cons x t ih =>
52	      rw [List.flatMap_cons, List.length_append, loadSlotI_length, ih, List.length_cons,
53	          Nat.add_comm t.length 1]
54	
55	theorem marshalI_length (args : List Nat) : (marshalI args).length = args.length := by
56	  rw [marshalI, length_flatMap_loadSlotI, List.length_zipIdx]
57	
58	/-- Instruction count of a statement's lowering — position/label-independent

61	    `Compile.lower`); the control-flow overheads are read off the compiler's
62	    layout: `ife` = 2 slot-loads + branch + jmp (4), `while` = +1 back-edge (5).
63	    `cref`/`clen` are `0` here (placeholder — `emit` still stubs them). -/
64	def csize : PStmt → Nat
65	  | .seq a b        => csize a + csize b
66	  | .ret            => 1
67	  | .brkB _         => 1
68	  | .contL _        => 1
69	  | .block body     => csize body
70	  | .ife _ _ _ t e  => 4 + csize e + csize t
71	  | .while _ _ _ b  => 5 + csize b
72	  | .clen rd _      => 3 + (if rd = 0 then 0 else 1)   -- synthConst (3) + storeSlot
73	  | .cref rd _      => 5 + (if rd = 0 then 0 else 1)   -- pc-read + synth + add + store

266	/-! ## §6 `hfn`/`hem` foundation — the prologue/epilogue resolve correspondence.
267	
268	    `hfn`/`hem` state that the compiler's RESOLVED stream (`compileProgT`'s output,
269	    = `L.instrs`), sliced at each function's byte position, equals
270	    `prologueI gd ++ emitCF … gd.body ++ epilogueI gd` (RESUME-CALL §C4). The full
271	    correspondence needs the `layout`-flatten structural arithmetic (which slice of
272	    `L.instrs` a function occupies) AND the `lower`↔`emitCF` label-resolution
273	    induction (the crux). This section closes the two SELF-CONTAINED halves that
274	    need neither: the machine `resolveOne` over `Compile.prologue`/`epilogue`
275	    (both all-`.ins`, position-independent) flattens to exactly `prologueI`/
276	    `epilogueI`. These are the leaf inputs the per-function `hfn` assembly consumes.
277	
278	    ⚠ **STILL OWED for `hfn`/`hem` (the big one, next session):** (a) the
279	    `layout`/`layoutItems` position arithmetic tying `fnPos g` to the slice of the
280	    resolved stream, and (b) `resolveOne`-over-`lower dat [] [] epi gd.body`
281	    = `emitCF P.data dpos fnPos [] [] epiPos bodyPos gd.body` — the label-resolution
282	    induction (the `matchesRealProg` #guard is its decidable shadow). -/
283	
284	open LowIR.Compile (prologue epilogue layoutItems resolveOne symSize storeSlot loadSlot
285	                    SymInstr A SP RA T0 T1 totalFrame maxRegF slotOff)
286	
287	/-- The instruction(s) a `.ins`/`.label` `SymInstr` resolves to (position-free):
288	    `.ins i ↦ [i]`, everything else `↦ []` (only `.label`, in a well-formed
289	    all-`.ins`/`.label` stream). -/
290	def insUnwrap : SymInstr → List Instr
291	  | .ins i => [i]
292	  | _      => []
293	
294	/-- A `SymInstr` is a concrete instruction or a (0-byte) label marker — the two
295	    cases `resolveOne` handles position-independently. -/
296	def isInsOrLabel : SymInstr → Bool
297	  | .ins _   => true
298	  | .label _ => true
299	  | _        => false
300	
301	/-- For a stream of only `.ins`/`.label` items, `resolveOne` is position-independent
302	    and maps each item to its `insUnwrap`. -/
303	theorem resolve_ins_mapM (lbls fns dats : List _) :
304	    ∀ (items : List SymInstr) (pos : Nat),
305	      items.all isInsOrLabel = true →
306	      (layoutItems items pos).1.mapM (resolveOne lbls fns dats)
307	        = some (items.map insUnwrap) := by
308	  intro items
309	  induction items with
310	  | nil => intro pos _; rfl
311	  | cons si rest ih =>
312	    intro pos hall
313	    simp only [List.all_cons, Bool.and_eq_true] at hall
314	    obtain ⟨hsi, hrest⟩ := hall
315	    cases si with
316	    | label l =>
317	      have hflat : (layoutItems (.label l :: rest) pos).1
318	          = (pos, SymInstr.label l) :: (layoutItems rest pos).1 := by
319	        simp only [layoutItems]
320	      rw [hflat, List.mapM_cons]
321	      simp only [show resolveOne lbls fns dats (pos, SymInstr.label l) = some [] from rfl,
322	                 ih pos hrest, List.map_cons, insUnwrap]
323	      rfl
324	    | ins i =>
325	      have hflat : (layoutItems (.ins i :: rest) pos).1
326	          = (pos, SymInstr.ins i) :: (layoutItems rest (pos + symSize (.ins i))).1 := by
327	        simp only [layoutItems]
328	      rw [hflat, List.mapM_cons]
329	      simp only [show resolveOne lbls fns dats (pos, SymInstr.ins i) = some [i] from rfl,
330	                 ih _ hrest, List.map_cons, insUnwrap]

252	def wf (P : Program) : Nat → Nat → Stmt → Bool
253	  | bD, lD, .seq a b          => wf P bD lD a && wf P bD lD b
254	  | bD, lD, .ife _ _ _ t e    => wf P bD lD t && wf P bD lD e
255	  | bD, lD, .while _ _ _ body => wf P bD (lD+1) body
256	  | bD, lD, .block body       => wf P (bD+1) lD body
257	  | bD, _,  .brkB k           => k < bD
258	  | _,  lD, .contL k          => k < lD
259	  | _,  _,  .slli _ _ sh      => sh < 64
260	  | _,  _,  .srli _ _ sh      => sh < 64
261	  | _,  _,  .call argc rvc f _ _ =>
262	      match List.lookup f P.env with
263	      | some fd => fd.argc == argc && fd.rvc == rvc
264	      | none    => false
265	  | _,  _,  .cref _ d         => (List.lookup d P.data).isSome
266	  | _,  _,  .clen _ d         => (List.lookup d P.data).isSome
267	  | _,  _,  _                 => true
268	
269	/-- Program well-formedness: every body is `wf` with NO enclosing block/loop
270	    (an escaping brk/cont at function level is ill-formed), no function binds
271	    its `frameReg` as a parameter (frameReg would shadow the param binding),
272	    and data object names are unique. -/
273	def wfProgram (P : Program) : Bool :=
274	  P.env.all (fun nf =>
275	    wf P 0 0 nf.2.body && !(nf.2.params.toList.contains nf.2.frameReg))
276	  && (P.data.map Prod.fst).eraseDups.length == P.data.length
277	
278	/-- Data-free convenience (pre-data name, used by the existing tests). -/
279	def wfEnv (env : Env) : Bool := wfProgram env
280	
281	/-! ### Data-segment layout — the SINGLE source of truth.
282	
283	    Both altitudes consume these: the IL harness places `dataSegment` at a
284	    chosen base (`installData`) with addresses from `dataOffsetsFrom 0`
285	    (`dbaseOf`); the compiler appends the SAME `dataSegment` to the blob with
286	    addresses from `dataOffsetsFrom segStart`. There is no second layout
287	    convention to keep in sync — and `dataSegment_at`/`installData_at` below
288	    PROVE the offsets↔bytes correspondence once, for both. -/
289	
290	/-- Round up to 8. -/
291	def pad8 (n : Nat) : Nat := (n + 7) / 8 * 8

59	def emit : PStmt → List Instr
60	  | .skip            => []
61	  | .annot _         => []
62	  | .addi rd rs imm  => loadSlotI rs T0 ++ [.addi T0 T0 imm] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
63	  | .add  rd r1 r2   => loadSlotI r1 T0 ++ loadSlotI r2 T1 ++ [.add T0 T0 T1] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
64	  | .sub  rd r1 r2   => loadSlotI r1 T0 ++ loadSlotI r2 T1 ++ [.sub T0 T0 T1] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
65	  | .orr  rd r1 r2   => loadSlotI r1 T0 ++ loadSlotI r2 T1 ++ [.or  T0 T0 T1] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
66	  | .slli rd rs sh   => loadSlotI rs T0 ++ [.slli T0 T0 sh] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
67	  | .srli rd rs sh   => loadSlotI rs T0 ++ [.srli T0 T0 sh] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
68	  | .lbu  rd rs imm  => loadSlotI rs T0 ++ [.lbu T0 T0 imm] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
69	  | .ld   rd rs imm  => loadSlotI rs T0 ++ [.ld  T0 T0 imm] ++ storeSlotI rd T0
70	  | .sb   rb rv imm  => loadSlotI rb T0 ++ loadSlotI rv T1 ++ [.sb T0 T1 imm]
71	  | .sd   rb rv imm  => loadSlotI rb T0 ++ loadSlotI rv T1 ++ [.sd T0 T1 imm]
72	  | .seq a b         => emit a ++ emit b
73	  | _                => []
74	
75	/-! ## `Emitted` — the static "code segment carries `is` at `pos`" fact. -/
76	
77	/-- `L.instrs` has `is` as its segment starting at byte offset `pos`
78	    (`pos` 4-aligned; each `is[j]` is `L.instrs[pos/4 + j]`). Static in `L`; the
79	    machine fetch is recovered from `Installed` (a `StInv` conjunct). -/
80	def Emitted (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is : List Instr) : Prop :=
81	  pos % 4 = 0 ∧ ∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < is.length),
82	    ∃ (h2 : pos / 4 + j < L.instrs.length), L.instrs[pos / 4 + j] = is[j]'h
83	
84	/-- `Emitted` of a concatenation splits: `is₁` at `pos`, `is₂` at `pos + 4|is₁|`. -/
85	theorem Emitted_append_left (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is₁ is₂ : List Instr)
86	    (h : Emitted L pos (is₁ ++ is₂)) : Emitted L pos is₁ := by
87	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := h
88	  refine ⟨ha, fun j hj => ?_⟩
89	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf j (by simp only [List.length_append]; omega)
90	  exact ⟨h2, by rw [he, List.getElem_append_left hj]⟩
91	
92	theorem Emitted_append_right (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is₁ is₂ : List Instr)
93	    (h : Emitted L pos (is₁ ++ is₂)) : Emitted L (pos + 4 * is₁.length) is₂ := by
94	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := h
95	  refine ⟨by omega, fun j hj => ?_⟩
96	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf (is₁.length + j) (by simp only [List.length_append]; omega)
97	  have hpos : (pos + 4 * is₁.length) / 4 + j = pos / 4 + (is₁.length + j) := by omega
98	  have hb : (pos + 4 * is₁.length) / 4 + j < L.instrs.length := by rw [hpos]; exact h2
99	  refine ⟨hb, ?_⟩
100	  simp only [hpos, he]
101	  simp
102	
103	/-- The fetch bridge: `Emitted` (static) + `Installed` (a `StInv` conjunct,
104	    preserved across steps) ⇒ the machine at `codeBase + (pos + 4j)` decodes to
105	    `is[j]`. This is how the op proofs read each instruction from memory. -/
106	theorem decode_emitted (L : Layout) (m : State) (pos : Nat) (is : List Instr)
107	    (hem : Emitted L pos is) (hinst : Installed L m) (j : Nat) (hj : j < is.length) :
108	    decode (fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (pos + 4 * j) }) = is[j]'hj := by
109	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := hem
110	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf j hj
111	  obtain ⟨hcode, _⟩ := hinst
112	  have hpc4 : L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (pos + 4 * j)
113	            = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * (pos / 4 + j)) := by
114	    congr 1; congr 1; omega
115	  rw [hpc4, ← he]; exact hcode (pos / 4 + j) h2

118	
119	theorem step_addi (m : State) (rd rs : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
120	    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .addi rd rs imm) :
121	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
122	  simp only [step, h]

/-! ## §3.3 — the program-level payoff `prog_sim` (statement; proof deferred).

    `lower_sim`/`call_sim` (§3.2/§3.3) are the induction's workhorses; their
    statements need the compile-time `Emitted` predicate (byte-stream ↔ lowering
    + resolved label addresses) that Phase 2 (AsmFacts) characterizes and the
    Phase 4.1 VERTICAL SLICE validates against the differential oracle — the
    go/no-go checkpoint for this whole relation. They land there, not guessed
    here. `prog_sim` below is self-contained (no `Emitted`) and is the corollary
    every ProgLib function composes with. -/

/-- **`prog_sim`** — if the D7/D8 IL says `entry(args)` computes `s'` (with the
    P1 padding `userPad`), then the compiled RV64I blob, started at `codeBase`
    with `args` in `a0..` and `sp = sp0`, runs to the halt pad in a state whose
    `a0..` hold `entry`'s return values and whose memory agrees with `s'`
    everywhere outside the blob and the stack. Const data is placed at
    `codeBase + segStart` on BOTH sides (`dataOffsetsFrom_shift`). -/
theorem prog_sim
    {P : Program} {entry : Name} {fd : FunDef} {args : List Word}
    {stackLo sp0 : Word} {fuel : Nat} {s' : St} {L : Layout} {m0 : State}
    (hlk    : List.lookup entry P.env = some fd)
    (hL     : layoutOf P entry L.codeBase L.stackLo = some L)
    (hpre   : SimPre L stackLo sp0)
    (hpc    : m0.pc = L.codeBase)
    (hsp    : m0.rget 2 = sp0)
    (hargs  : ∀ i, i < fd.argc → m0.rget (10 + i) = args.getD i 0)
    (hinst  : Installed L m0)
    (hmem   : ∀ a, ¬ MachPriv L [] a →
                installData (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) P.data (fun _ => 0) a
                  = m0.mem a)
    (hrun   : LowIR.Prog.run P stackLo fuel entry args (fun _ => 0) sp0
                (userPad P.env) (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) = some s') :
    ∃ k, (Rv64i.runFuel L.haltPc k m0).pc = L.haltPc
       ∧ (∀ j, j < fd.rvc →
            (Rv64i.runFuel L.haltPc k m0).rget (10 + j) = s'.rget (fd.rets.toList.getD j 0))
       ∧ (∀ a, ¬ memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen → ¬ MachStack stackLo sp0 a →
            s'.mem a = (Rv64i.runFuel L.haltPc k m0).mem a) := by
  sorry

/-! ## Executable sanity (`#guard`) — the def oracle (RESUME-PROGSIM §6). -/

Here's what remains in the `compile_sim`-for-Prog campaign, grounded in the current tree. There is exactly **one statement-level `sorry` left**: `prog_sim` (`Defs.lean:491`). Everything else is Phase-2 plumbing that discharges the flat hypotheses `lower_sim_cf`/`prog_sim` take.

## The flat obligations `lower_sim_cf` takes

`lower_sim_cf` (the whole-statement simulation) is **proved and axiom-clean**, but parameterized on compile-time facts the real layout must supply:

| Obligation | Status |
|---|---|
| `hstackLo`, `hdat`, `hdbase`, `hpad` | ✅ discharged (`887f3d7`, `userPad_eq`) |
| **`hfn`/`hem`** — per-function `Emitted` correspondence | 🔨 crux done (`lower_resolve`, today); **glue owed** |
| `hdpos` (blob-size bound), `halign` (codeBase % 4 = 0) | ⬜ thread in at `prog_sim` |

## 1. Finish `hfn`/`hem` (the current front)

`lower_resolve` (just landed) proves `resolveOne ∘ layoutItems ∘ lower = emitCF` for a body **in isolation**. To get `hfn`/`hem` — that `L.instrs` (the real `compileProgT` output) carries `prologueI g ++ emitCF … g.body ++ epilogueI g` at each `fnPos g` — three pieces remain:

- **Layout position arithmetic**: tie `fnPos g` to the correct *slice* of the resolved stream — the `layout`/`layoutItems` flatten over `segs` (`compileProgT:322-324`). This is the "which bytes does function g occupy" bookkeeping.
- **Discharge `lower_resolve`'s premises** from the *global* tables: `LEnvOk`/`LblConsistent`/`TabOk` from the real `layout`/`fnTab`/`dataOffsets`. The load-bearing lemma here is **`fresh`-monotonicity ⇒ label-nodup** (internal labels are globally unique, so their `lbls` entries are the layout positions) — this is the remaining non-trivial plumbing.
- **Glue per function**: compose the above with the already-proven `prologue_resolves`/`epilogue_resolves` (`AsmFacts §6`) to assemble the full `FnEmitted` bundle.

## 2. `hdpos` / `halign`

Small: `dposOf d < 2²⁰` (a blob-size bound, from `SimPre`) and `codeBase % 4 = 0` (a `prog_sim` hypothesis). Neither is standalone — they thread in at `prog_sim`.

## 3. Phase 6 — `prog_sim` itself (`Defs.lean:491`)

The program-level payoff. Composes:
- the entry-stub `jal ra, entry` step (offset 0) + `sp`/args setup,
- `lower_sim_cf` on the **entry function's body** (discharging all flat obligations from `hfn`/`hem`/`SimPre`/`Installed`),
- the halt-pad bridge (`jal x0, 0` self-loop at offset 4 = `haltPc`),
- and the exit-state memory/return-value agreement.

This is the corollary every `ProgLib` function (strlen/strtoull/hex0/hex1) then instantiates.

---

**Bottom line:** the hard proof-engineering (the `call` case, then `lower_resolve`) is behind us. What's left is (1) the layout position bookkeeping + label-nodup to finish `hfn`/`hem`, then (2) `prog_sim` assembly. After that, `compile_sim` for Prog is closed and the post-campaign ladder (Prog frame theorem → `hex0F` at Prog → SSA→Prog → borrow checker) opens up.

40	
41	/-! ## §3.1 (part) — where the code and data live: `Layout` and `Installed` -/
42	
43	/-- The compiled artifact's placement, per RESUME-PROGSIM §3.1: the base
44	    address, the resolved instruction stream (`= (compileProgT P entry).1`), the
45	    function→byte-offset table, the byte offset where the data segment starts
46	    inside the blob (`pad8 (4 * #instrs)` — every instruction is 4 bytes after
47	    resolution), and the program's const data. -/
48	structure Layout where
49	  codeBase : Word
50	  instrs   : List Instr
51	  fnTab    : List (Name × Nat)
52	  segStart : Nat
53	  data     : Data
54	  stackLo  : Word          -- the stack floor (a fixed per-run constant, like
55	                           -- `codeBase`); the free stack `[stackLo, sp)` below the
56	                           -- current frame is machine-private (C2 — see `StInv`).
57	deriving Repr
58	
59	/-- Build the `Layout` for `entry` at `codeBase` straight from the FROZEN
60	    compiler. `none` iff the program is uncompilable (same condition as
61	    `compileProgT`). `segStart` is recomputed here from the resolved stream
62	    and is DEFINITIONALLY the compiler's `pad8 codeEnd` (proved in Phase 2). -/
63	def layoutOf (P : Program) (entry : Name) (codeBase stackLo : Word) : Option Layout :=
64	  match compileProgT P entry with
65	  | some (is, fns, _) =>
66	      some { codeBase, instrs := is, fnTab := fns,
67	             segStart := pad8 (4 * is.length), data := P.data, stackLo }
68	  | none => none
69	
70	/-- The machine halt pc: the self-loop landing pad the compiler puts at
71	    `codeBase+4` (the entry stub is `jal ra,entry` at `codeBase`; the entry
72	    returns to `codeBase+4`). Run the machine with `runFuel L.haltPc`. -/
73	def Layout.haltPc (L : Layout) : Word := L.codeBase + 4
74	
75	/-- Address one past the last instruction — the code window's top. -/
76	def Layout.codeEnd (L : Layout) : Word :=
77	  L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * L.instrs.length)
78	
79	/-- `Installed L m` — the compiled blob is faithfully present in `m`'s memory:
80	    (code) the 32-bit word at `codeBase + 4j` decodes via the TRUSTED
81	    `Rv64i.decode` to `instrs[j]`; (data) the const-data byte at
82	    `codeBase + segStart + i` equals `dataSegment data`'s byte `i`
83	    (`Prog.dataSegment_at` already relates that segment to the object bytes). -/
84	def Installed (L : Layout) (m : State) : Prop :=
85	  (∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < L.instrs.length),
86	      Rv64i.decode (Rv64i.fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * j) })
87	        = L.instrs[j]'h)
88	  ∧ (∀ (i : Nat) (h : i < (dataSegment L.data).length),
89	      m.mem (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (L.segStart + i)) = (dataSegment L.data)[i]'h)
90	
91	/-! ### Computable shadows of `Installed` (the #guard oracle). -/
92	
93	/-- Every instruction slot decodes correctly (computable form of `Installed`'s
94	    code conjunct). -/
95	def codeInstalledB (L : Layout) (m : State) : Bool :=
96	  (List.range L.instrs.length).all fun j =>
97	    Rv64i.decode (Rv64i.fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * j) })
98	      == L.instrs.getD j .unknown
99	
100	/-- Every data byte is present (computable form of `Installed`'s data conjunct). -/
101	def dataInstalledB (L : Layout) (m : State) : Bool :=
102	  (List.range (dataSegment L.data).length).all fun i =>
103	    m.mem (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (L.segStart + i))
104	      == (dataSegment L.data).getD i 0
105

# PROGRESS — LowIR & libc-formalize

## 2026-07-06 (compile_sim campaign) — Phase 2: `LowerFacts` layer 3 — `lower_resolve` DONE

The correspondence induction is closed: resolving (`resolveOne` over `layoutItems`)
the compiler's laid-out symbolic body stream flattens to exactly `emitCF` at the
same byte position, for ALL 22 statement constructors. `#print axioms
lower_resolve = [propext, Quot.sound]`; full `lake build` green. This was the crux
of `hfn`/`hem` (the `matchesRealProg` #guard is its decidable shadow, now a
theorem for every program).

Statement (structural induction on `stmt`, threading three consistency relations):
```
lower_resolve (dat P dpos fnPos lbls fns dats) (htab : TabOk dpos fnPos fns dats) :
  ∀ stmt bs cs epi cnt here bp cp ep r,
    (layoutItems (lower dat bs cs epi stmt cnt).1 here).1.mapM (resolveOne lbls fns dats) = some r →
    Prog.wf P bs.length cs.length stmt = true →
    LEnvOk lbls bs cs epi bp cp ep →
    LblConsistent lbls (layoutItems (lower dat bs cs epi stmt cnt).1 here).2.1 →
    r.flatten = emitCF dat dpos fnPos bp cp ep here stmt
````

192	  let tf := totalFrame fd
193	  let params := fd.params.toList
194	  [.ins (.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofInt 12 (-(tf : Int)))),
195	   .ins (.sd SP RA 0)]
196	  ++ params.zipIdx.flatMap (fun pi => storeSlot pi.1 (A pi.2))
197	  ++ ((List.range (maxRegF fd + 1)).filter
198	        (fun r => r != 0 && !params.contains r && r != fd.frameReg)).map

200	  ++ (if fd.frameReg = 0 then [] else
201	        [.ins (.addi T0 SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd)))] ++ storeSlot fd.frameReg T0)
202	  ++ (List.range (fd.frameSize / 8)).map
203	       (fun i => .ins (.sd SP 0 (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd + 8 * i))))

205	/-- Epilogue (jumped to by `ret`, fallen into on `normal`): rets → a0..,
206	    restore ra + sp, return. -/
207	def epilogue (fd : FunDef) : List SymInstr :=
208	  fd.rets.toList.zipIdx.flatMap (fun ri => loadSlot ri.1 (A ri.2))
209	  ++ [.ins (.ld RA SP 0),
210	      .ins (.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (totalFrame fd))),
211	      .ins (.jalr 0 RA 0)]

214	def compileFun (dat : Data) (fd : FunDef) : M (List SymInstr) := do
215	  let epi ← fresh

219	/-! ### Layout & resolution -/
220	
221	/-- Emitted size of a symbolic instruction (fixed per constructor — layout
222	    must be value-independent). `cref` = jal-pc-read + 3-instr delta synth +
223	    add (see `resolveOne`). -/
224	def symSize : SymInstr → Nat
225	  | .label _ => 0
226	  | .cref _  => 20
227	  | _        => 4
228	
229	/-- Pass A over one stream from byte position `pos`: positioned items,
230	    label→addr entries, next position. -/
231	def layoutItems : List SymInstr → Nat → List (Nat × SymInstr) × List (Nat × Nat) × Nat
232	  | [], pos => ([], [], pos)
233	  | .label l :: rest, pos =>
234	      let (flat, lbls, pos') := layoutItems rest pos
235	      ((pos, .label l) :: flat, (l, pos) :: lbls, pos')
236	  | si :: rest, pos =>
237	      let (flat, lbls, pos') := layoutItems rest (pos + symSize si)
238	      ((pos, si) :: flat, lbls, pos')
239	
240	/-- Pass A: walk the whole program, assigning byte positions. Returns the flat
241	    positioned stream, the label→addr map, the function→addr map, and the end
242	    position (total code bytes). -/
243	def layout : List (Name × List SymInstr) →
244	    (start : Nat) →
245	    List (Nat × SymInstr) × List (Nat × Nat) × List (Name × Nat) × Nat
246	  | [], pos => ([], [], [], pos)
247	  | (n, items) :: rest, pos =>
248	      let (flat1, lbls1, pos') := layoutItems items pos
249	      let (flat2, lbls2, fns, endP) := layout rest pos'
250	      (flat1 ++ flat2, lbls1 ++ lbls2, (n, pos) :: fns, endP)
251	
252	/-- Resolve one positioned symbolic instruction (range-checked). `cref d`:
253	    `jal T0, +4` reads the pc (T0 := address of the next instruction — pc-read
254	    with NO auipc, staying inside the 16-encoding surface and keeping the blob
255	    position-independent), then a fixed 3-instr synth of the delta to the data
256	    object into T1, then `add T0, T0, T1`. -/
257	def resolveOne (lbls : List (Nat × Nat)) (fns : List (Name × Nat))
258	    (dats : List (Name × Nat)) :
259	    Nat × SymInstr → Option (List Instr)
260	  | (_,   .label _)      => some []
261	  | (_,   .ins i)        => some [i]
262	  | (pos, .cref d)       => do
263	      let off ← List.lookup d dats
264	      let δ : Int := (off : Int) - ((pos : Int) + 4)
265	      let lo : Int := ((δ + 2048) % 4096) - 2048
266	      let hi : Int := (δ - lo) / 4096
267	      if -2048 ≤ hi && hi ≤ 2047 then
268	        some [.jal T0 (BitVec.ofInt 21 4),
269	              .addi T1 0 (BitVec.ofInt 12 hi), .slli T1 T1 12,
270	              .addi T1 T1 (BitVec.ofInt 12 lo),
271	              .add T0 T0 T1]
272	      else none
273	  | (pos, .br c a b l)   => do
274	      let tgt ← List.lookup l lbls
275	      let δ : Int := (tgt : Int) - (pos : Int)
276	      if -4096 ≤ δ && δ ≤ 4094 then some [condInstr c a b δ] else none
277	  | (pos, .jmp l)        => do
278	      let tgt ← List.lookup l lbls
279	      let δ : Int := (tgt : Int) - (pos : Int)
280	      if -(2^20 : Int) ≤ δ && δ ≤ (2^20 : Int) - 2 then some [jal0 δ] else none
281	  | (pos, .callf f)      => do
282	      let tgt ← List.lookup f fns
283	      let δ : Int := (tgt : Int) - (pos : Int)
284	      if -(2^20 : Int) ≤ δ && δ ≤ (2^20 : Int) - 2
285	      then some [.jal RA (BitVec.ofInt 21 δ)] else none
286	
287	def pad8 (n : Nat) : Nat := (n + 7) / 8 * 8
288

309	def compileProgT (P : Program) (entry : Name) :
310	    Option (List Instr × List (Name × Nat) × List (Name × Nat)) :=
311	  if !(wfProgram P && P.env.all (fun nf => fnOk nf.2)
312	       && (List.lookup entry P.env).isSome
313	       && P.data.all (fun d => d.2.length < 2 ^ 22))    -- cref/clen synth range
314	       -- NOTE: bound tightened 2^23 → 2^22 (2026-07-05). `clen` synthesizes the
315	       -- data length via `synthConst` (no range check), whose hi immediate is
316	       -- `synthHi len = ⌊(len+2048)/4096⌋`; that hits 2048 for `len ∈ [2^23−2048,
317	       -- 2^23)`, overflowing `BitVec.ofInt 12` (wraps to −2048 ⇒ wrong length).
318	       -- 2^22 sits well inside the synthesizable band; discharges ProgSim `hdat`.
319	  then none
320	  else
321	    let segs : List (Name × List SymInstr) :=
322	      (P.env.mapM (fun nf => do pure (nf.1, ← compileFun P.data nf.2)) : M _).run' 0
323	    let (flat, lbls, fns, codeEnd) :=
324	      layout (("", [.callf entry, .ins (jal0 0)]) :: segs) 0
325	    -- SAME layout function as the IL harness (`Prog.dbaseOf`/`installData`
326	    -- use `dataOffsetsFrom 0`); correspondence proved in `Prog.dataSegment_at`.
327	    let dats := dataOffsetsFrom (pad8 codeEnd) P.data
328	    ((flat.mapM (resolveOne lbls fns dats)).map List.flatten).map
329	      (fun is => (is, fns.filter (fun f => f.1 != ""), dats))

I've made major progress on the hfn/hem glue this session. Here's where things stand.

## What landed (8 axiom-clean commits, all `[propext, Quot.sound]`, full build green)

Starting from `lower_resolve` (the `lower`↔`emitCF` correspondence, done earlier this session), I built out the entire new module `LowIR/ProgSim/LayoutFacts.lean`:

**Position arithmetic & composition**
- `resolveOne_length` / `resolve_length` — the byte-position ↔ instruction-index bridge (`4·#instrs = totalSymSize`).
- `layout_end` / `layout_flat_append` / `layout_fns_append` — the layout pass distributes over segment-list append.
- **`compileFun_resolves`** (the payoff) — resolving a function's stream = `prologueI ++ emitCF … body ++ epilogueI`, composing `prologue_resolves` + `lower_resolve` + `epilogue_resolves`.

**The global label-nodup story (fully done)** — this was the hard, risky part:
- `lower_labels_range` / `lower_labels_nodup` / `lower_snd_ge` — per-`lower` label ranges + distinctness.
- `compileFun_labels_*` — lifted through `compileFun` (epilogue label `= c`, body labels `≥ c+1`).
- `mapSegs_labels` — the crux induction over the fresh-counter-threaded segment `mapM`: disjoint `[cᵢ, cᵢ₊₁)` ranges ⇒ the whole program's labels are `Nodup`.
- `layout_lbls_keys` + `lookup_of_nodup_mem` + **`global_lbls_nodup` / `lbls_lookup`** — any `(l,p)` in the global label table is looked up to exactly `p`.

Along the way I chased down three Mathlib-free Classical.choice leaks (conjunction-`omega`, bare `simp` catch-alls, and — the subtle one — `beq_self_eq_true`/`List.lookup` on Nat keys pulling Classical via Nat's `LawfulBEq`; `rw [beq_iff_eq]` is the clean route). All recorded in memory.

## What remains for a complete `hfn`/`hem` (assembly only — the hard machinery now exists)
1. **Position-membership**: show each function's body + epilogue labels sit in the global table at their absolute byte positions (a `layoutItems` label-position lemma threaded through `layout_flat_append`/`layout_fns_append`). This feeds `lbls_lookup` to discharge `compileFun_resolves`' `hepi`/`hlc`.
2. **Flat resolve split** at each `fnPos g` (task 5) to place each slice.
3. `TabOk` from the `dposOf`/`fnPosOf` defs (≈ definitional).
4. Assemble `hfn`/`hem`, then `hdpos`/`halign`, then Phase 6 `prog_sim` (the lone remaining `sorry`, Defs.lean:491).

`docs/PROGRESS.md` and the `progsim-campaign-frontier` memory are updated with the precise remaining plan.
